3a1(1): @##//can gdul ba dang /rnam par dag pa'i chos kyis sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba yongs su ston pa dang /'gro ba thams cad yongs su bskyab par byang 
3a2(1): chub sems dpa'i rtse gcig tu smon laM btab pas skyil mo krung bcas te 'khod do//byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad kyang 'di lta ste/chags pa med par spyod pas/sangs 
3a3(1): rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du 'gro ba dang /'di lta ste gnas pa med pa'i lus dang /'di lta ste gnas ba med pa'i lus dang /'dilta ste tshad med pa'i lus kyis kun du 'gro ba'i 
3a4(1): ye shes kyi lus kyi byin gyis rlabs dang /'di lta ste mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'khrul ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa la 'jug pa dang /'di lta ste 
3a5(1): thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgo 'byam klas pa tshad med par gyur pa dang /'di lta ste chos la mi 'jigs par mkhas pa'i snang ba mtha' yas pa dang /'di lta ste phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal par 
3a6(1): mi zad pa'i so so yang dag par rig pa'i tshul bshad pa dang /'di lta ste gzungs thams cad kyi snang bar byas pa'i ye shes kyi phung po rab tu rgyas pa dang /'di lta ste chosakyi dbyings 
3b1(1): rgyas par gyur pa'i shes rab kyi mig rnam par dag pa'i spobs pa dang /'di lta ste 'di lta nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas par ye shes kyi spyod yul yongs su gnas pa dang /mtshams med cing 
3b2(1): mtha' yas pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis spyod pa dang ldan pa rab tu thob par gyur pa dag go/ji ltar gling bzhi pa 'di dga' ldan gyi gnas su byang chub sems dpa' 'dus par gyur pa de bzhin du 
3b3(1): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa dang /dga' ldan gyi gnas dag tu yang byang chub sems dpa'i ming de skad ces bya ba dag 'jig rten gyi khams ming de skad ces bya ba dang 
3b4(1): nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan de skad ces bya ba dag gi zham ring na tshangs par spyod pa sha stag 'ongs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis zhabs pus me'i lhang gnyis nas 'od gzer 
3b5(1): bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong byung ste/phyogs bcu thams cad snang bar byas nas/'jig rten gyi khams thams chad bstan to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams
3b6(1): de dag gi gling bzhi pa thams cad dang /dga' ldan gyi gnad thams cad na de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa yod par mthong bar gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' de dang ji snyed kyis 
3b7(1): phyogs bcur chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi mthar thug pa dag na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par rol pa mthong ba gyur pa de dag thams cad de bzhin
3b8(1): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad kyi sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la dge ba'i rtsa ba'i grogs su gyur pa dag ste/'di ltar sangs 
4a1(1): @#//rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa'i zab mos rnam par 'phrul pa la mos pa sha stag go/chos kyi dbyings kyi lus tha dad pa med pa'i rjes su song ba sha stag go/gnas med pa'i sangs 
4a2(1): rgyas kyi zhing du 'gro ba'i rjes su song bas rab tu zhugs pa sha stag go/sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab pa blta ba la rjes su gzhol ba sha stag go/chags pa med pa'i sems skad cig gis chos   
4a3(1): kyi dbyings khyab par byed pa sha stag go/bsam pa rin po che rdul dang bral ba dang ldan pa sha stag go/brjod du med pa'i sangs rgyas mang po dang 'grogs pa dang ldan pa sha stag go//sangs rgyas kyi 
4a4(1): byin gyis rlabs grangs med pa dang ldan pa sha stag goyang dag par spyod pa'i mchog gi pha rol tu phyin pa sha stag go/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi dran pa rnam par dag  
4a5(1): pa la gnas pa sha stag go/blo dang sems skad cig thams cad kyis cher 'phags pa rab tu thob pa sha stag go/chu ngur zhugs shing /cher gyur pa'i spyod pa la dbang ba sha stag go/cher gyur 
4a6(1): pa la zhugs shing chu ngur 'jug pa'i rjes su chud nas ye shes kyis spyod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa sha stags go/de lta bu'i gnas dang sangs rgyas kyi sku'i rang bzhin rjes su rtogs pa dang //
4a7(1): de bzhin gshegs pa dang 'grogs shing thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang /rin po che sna tshogs ltar grub pa'i lus dang /de bzhin gshegs  
4a8(1): par 'gyur ba'i spyod yul thams cad du 'gro ba dang /ye shes chen po'i rgyud kyi sgo rnam par bye ba dang /ye shes kyi stobs rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi mthu'i mchog thob pa dang /rdo rje'i 
4b1(1): gcod pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul rab tu thob pa dang /sems can bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong grangs med pa rnams yongs su smin par gdul ba rmongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi  
4b2(1): rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad du 'gro ba gdon mi za ba'i mchog thob pa dang /bgrang ba thams cad la mngon par chags pa dang /bgrang ba mngon par bsgrub pa thams cad shin tu bslabs 
4b3(1): pa dang bsgrang ba'i shes pa thams cad kyi mthar thug cing chos rnam par dgod pa la mkhas pa dang /'di dag dang gzhan yang yon tan bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa dang //
4b4(1): yon tan bsngags pa rnam par dag pa mi zad pas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis dus gsum gyi yon tan tshad med pa dang ldan pa sha stag/de bzhin gshegs pa mngon sum du bya ba  
4b5(1): dang sangs rgyas 'byam klas pa thams cad lta ba'i phyir kun nas 'dus par gyur to/de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyog+sa 
4b6(1): bcur rnam par bltas te de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//yang dag sangs rgyas skye ba med//rgyal ba mya ngan mi 'da' ste//rgyu mthun pa yi smon lam gyis//des ni  
4b7(1): rnam par 'phrul par snang //'di dag chos ni bsam mi khyab//'di dag sems kyi spyod yul min//gang gis ye shes pha rol phyin//des ni sangs rgyas spyod yul mthong //
4b8(1): lus 'di yang dag sangs rgyas min//sgra yang de bzhin gshegs pa min//lus dang sgra las gzhan na yang //sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul mi mthong //sngon gyi las rnams ma    
5a1(1): @#//gtogs par//rang byung chos kyi spyod yul gyi//mchog gi tshul rnams shes pa ni//blo gzhan rnams kyis mi nus so//yang dag sangs rgyas rnam 
5a2(1): 'phrul 'di//gang dag nas kyang ma 'ongs te//gang du'ang 'gro bar mi 'gyur ro//'di dag rang 'byung sku yin no//tshad med shin tu 'dren pa rnams//chos 'di rab tu 
5a3(1): bshad mdzad kyang //chos la gnas pa gang yang med//zhing rnams bye ba kun nas snang //mkhyen pas mtha' dang dbus med de//chos kun thugs su chud cing rtogs//
5a4(1): chos kyi dbyings la rab zhugs nas//rnam par 'phrul pa ston pa mdzad//chos rnams kun la chags pa med//'gro la de ni bsgribs mi mnga'//tshad med pa yi yon tan gyis//
5a5(1): zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //gang zhig ye shes 'di dag dang //ye shes thams cad rtogs 'dod pas//bsam pa rnam par sbyang byas te//byang chub spyod pa spyad par bya//
5a6(1): drang srong chen po'i byin gyi rlabs//'di 'dra gang gis mthong gyur cing //de ni the tshom mi byed na//dge bshes mchog la bsten pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 
5a7(1): dam pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//shin tu zab ste brjod 
5a8(1): du med//brjod du yod kyang 'da' bar dka'//tshig gi lam la shin tu 'das//nam mkha' la ni rgyal ba snang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin rlabs kyis//mi yi seng ge 
5b1(1): med pa yi//des ni sku 'di rab tu 'byung //thams cad gzigs kyi spyod yul gyi//ye shes chen po 'di 'dra ste//'di la gang zhig rtogs 'dod pa//des ni rgyal ba mngon sum byos//
5b2(1): yid kyi dbang po rnam dag pas//de bzhin gshegs pa bsnyen bkur zhing //'di yi tshul las 'da' byed che//nam yang skyo bar mi bya'o//mi zad pa yi bsod nams dang //byang chub    
5b3(1): sems kyis bstan pa rnams//the tshom rnam par bzlog byas te//de dag la ni skyo med ltos//rgyal sras gang gis chos dbyings kyis//shin tu bstan pa'i mthar phyin pa//
5b4(1): des ni kun gzigs rnam 'phrul ba//'di dag shin tu 'da' bar bya//dad pa chos 'dis rtsa ba zhes//chos kyi phung po bshad mdzad kyis//dad pa shin tu cher skyed la//byang 
5b5(1): chub spyod pa spyad par gyis//sangs rgyas bsti stang gang byed cing //de bzhin gshegs la drin bzo ba//yang dag sangs rgyas dpal ldan las//de dag rgyang mar mi gnaso//
5b6(1): bdag nyid che ba 'di lta bu//gang gis mthos thod mkhas pa rnams//spyod yul thob par cis 'gyur ba'i//smon lam su zhig spyod mi byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpa' 
5b7(1): ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltar nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od snang bas//
6a1(1): @#//mig gis gzugs rnams mthong 'gyur ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas byin rlabs kyis//dag pa'i sems ldan rgyal ba'i mthong //ji ltar brtson 'grus    
6a2(1): stobs ldan pa//rgya mtsho la ni 'jug par byed//de bzhin ye shes stobs ldan pas//bsam du med pa'i sangs rgyas mthong //ji ltar sa bon rnam par dag/zhing bzang la ni  
6a3(1): skyer 'gyur ba//de bzhin sems bsam rnam dag la//sangs rgyas chos ni skye bar 'gyur//ji ltar nor gyi phung po yis//dbul ba thams cad spong byed pa//de bzhin sangs 
6a4(1): rgyas chos rnams kyis//byang chung sems dpa' rnam dag 'gyur//ji ltar dug tu 'gyur pa la//nad med dug sman rgyal bar byed//de bzhin nyon mongs rnam pa la//rnam   
6a5(1): dag tshul ni spyod yul lo//sangs rgyas kyis ni gang bsngags pa'i//dge ba'i bshes la brten byas na//de yi mthus ni sangs rgyas kyi//chos nyid 'di dag thos par  
6a6(1): 'gyur /bskal pa bye ba grangs med par//sbyin pa mchog rnams sbyin byas kyang //'di yi tshul la rab spyod pa'i//grangs dang char yang mi 'gro'o//sangs yon tan mtha' 
6a7(1): yas te࿒ /mtshan gyis shin tu rnam par bris//brjod pa'i yang dag sangs rgyas rnams//rgyal bas shes par mi nus so//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog/
6a8(1): gnas nas g.yo ba mi mnga' bar//sku ni kun du rnam rgyu zhing //chos kyi dbyings ni ston pa mdzad//ji ltar nam mkha'i khams 'di ni//nam yang rang bzhin mi 'gyur 
6b1(1): te de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //ngo bo nyid las 'gyur ba med//den byang chub sems dpa' skar ma'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par 
6b2(1): ltas te de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//mi yi 'jig rten 'di kun dang //lha yi gnas rnams thams cad dang //'gro ba ma lus kun na yang //mi yi gtso bo shin tu snang //
6b3(1): ji ltar sems dngos gcig pu las//rnam pa tha dad sems brjod pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas sku gcig las//sangs rgyas thams cad brjod pa yin//byang chub gnyis su med 
6b4(1): par brjod//byang chub de yang mtshan nyid med//mtshan gyis mngon par rnam bris pa'i//sku rnams gnyis su snang bar gyur//mkha' dang chos kyi dbyings la zhugs//sgyu ma'i sa 
6b5(1): yang rab tu stan//de phyir sangs rgyas de dag gi/spyod pa lta ba zad pa med//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//mgon po thams cad dus gsum du//ji ltar 'dul ba 'gro ba la//
6b6(1): gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu snang //'di 'dra'i lus su bdag gyur ces//sangs rgyas rtog pa mi mdzad de//ston la rnam rtog mi mnga' yang //byin gyis rlabs ni ston par mdzad//
6b7(1): dbyer med pa yi dbyings la yang //chos kyi dbyings ni yang dag gnas//'jig rten khams ni tshad med par//thub pas rnam par rtog pa bstan//sku ni sprul pa ma yin te//
6b8(1): sprul min pa'ang sku ma yin//sprul pa med pa'i chos 'di yis//sprul pa rnams ni rab tu bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshad med pa//chos kyi dbyings la shin tu brten//
7a1(1): mtha' dang dbus med rab tu zab//tshig gi lam gyis bzla bar dka'//kun tu 'gro ba'i chos rnams la//de bzhin gshegs pa don nges mkhyen//
7a2(1): zhing rnams kun la chags mi mnga'//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par spyod//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs 
7a3(1): bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//kun mkhyen ye shes ma chags pa//de la bzhugs par gang gyur pa//de yi sems ni tshad mi zin//
7a4(1): byang chub kyi ni spyad pa spyod//de bzhin gshegs pa mang po rnams//yul dang yul du de dag snang //chos rnams phyogs gcig gang la yang //yang dag sangs rgyas gnas pa med//
7a5(1): 'jig rten sgron ma de dag gis//byin gyis rlabs ni gang bstan pa//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//'di yi mtha' ni rnyed par dka'//dus gsum rnams na ci snyed 
7a6(1): pa'i//sems can rnams kyi mthar thug pa//bgrang ba'i mthar ni phyin par nus//'dren pa la ni thar thug med//de dag sku ni gcig tu snang //sku gnyis de dag bsam mi 
7a7(1): khyab//'jig rten khams su snang gyur kyang //sku la gnyis su gyur pa med//ji ltar zla ba shar gyur pa//tshad med pa yi gzugs brnyan rnams//chu yi snod du snang gyur  
7a8(1): kyang //zla ba gnyis su gyur pa med/de bzhin chags med mkhyen ldan rnams//chags med rnam par 'tshang rgya ba//zhing rnams kun du rab tu snang //sangs rgyas la 
7b1(1): ni gnyis kyang med //'di la gcig kyang yong ma yin//sku ni gnyis sam gsum du med//ji ltar sems can bsam ba bzhin//tshad med par ni rab tu snang //sku ni 'das 
7b2(1): pa ma yin te//ma 'ongs pa yang sku ma yin//skyes dang byang chub mya ngan 'das//skad cig gi ni rab tu snang //ji ltar sgyu mar gyur pa'i gzugs//skyes pa ma yin 'ongs pa 
7b3(1): min /de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku//skye ba med pa shin tu bstan//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rin po che'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi(sa)
7b4(1): phyogs bcur rnam par ltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//yang dag sangs rgyas tshad med pas//tshad du sku yang rnam par bstan//ji ltar 'dul 
7b5(1): ba'i sems can rnams//de bzhin 'dren pa rab tu mthong //sku la phyogs ni med par brjod//sangs rgyas yul ni ma lus khyab//bar snang nam mkha' ji lta ba//
7b6(1): de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//der ni sems kyang 'pho ba med//sems las skye ba'ang yod pa min//sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyod yul ni/skye ba med la rab 
7b7(1): tu gnas//ji ltar rab rib ldan pa'i mig/nang na med cing phyi nanga' med//de bzhin 'jig rten 'jig rten sangs rgyas rnams//nang na mi bzhugs phyi na'ang med//
7b8(1): 'jig rten kun gyi don gyi phyir//rkang gnyis kyis ni mchog byung ste//'jig rten du ni ma byung yang //'jig rten dag tu skye bos mthong //sangs rgyas rnams la zhing 
8a1(1): @#//mi gnas//rgyal ba dag la nyin mtshan med//skad cig ma yin zla ba min//rgyal ba la ni lo mi gnas//'jig rten dag nas 'di skad brjod//nyi ma che 
8a2(1): ge mo zhig tu//rang byung byang chub rgyal bas thob//sangs rgyas nyi ma'i rjes mi 'brang //de la bskal pa yod ma yin//dus kyang ma lus med pa ste//dus gsum yang dag 
8a3(1): rdzogs sangs rgyas//mgon po rnams ni mnga' bar 'gyur//ji ltar nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//nams kyang mtshan dang mi gnas te//nyin mtshan zhes kyang rab tu brjod//
8a4(1): de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid do//bskal pa gcig dang dus gsum du//'dren pa bzhugs pa ma yin te//dus gsum zhes kyang rab tu brjod//sangs rgyas rnams kyi 
8a5(1): chos nyid ltos//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdul dang bral ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//
8a6(1): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//yang dag sangs rgyas sku gcig ste//'dren pa thams cad don cig kyang //tshad med gzhan dang gzhan dag tu//phyogs 
8a7(1): bcu kun na rab tu snang //gang gis ma lus thams cad kyi//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun khyab pa//sems can gtso bo thub pa yi//spyod yul rnams ni ji 'dra 
8a8(1): ltos//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos nyid ni//nang gi skyus kyang snang ba min//de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis brlabs//phyi rol skus kyang mi snang ngo //
8b1(1): dus mang kun tu las rnams kyis//ji ltar las rnaMs bsags pa bzhin//de bzhin so sor phye ba'i skus//rigs su byung bar rab tu bstan//rgyal ba'i chos nyid ma/
8b2(1): /gtogs par//sangs rgyas sku ni 'di 'dra bar//grangs su bgrang bar mi nus te//bsam par lta yang shes mi nus//ji ltar bdag cag sems kyi las//bsam gyis mi khyab 
8b3(1): mi 'byung ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//sems kyi 'phrin las mi mnga'o//ji ltar zhing rnams bsam yas pa//sa las rnam par dag par mdzes//de   
8b4(1): bozhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khya ba//'gro ba dag na mdzes par snang //ji ltar las rnams 'jug pa ni//rkyen gyis rab tu 'byung 'gyur ba//de bzhin rgyu rnams mthun  
8b5(1): pa las//sangs rgyas shin tu thong bar 'gyur//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal po ni//ji ltar yid bsam rdzogs byed pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //rtag tu smon   
8b6(1): lam rdzogs pa sgrub//mtha' dang dbus med 'jig rten 'dir//'jig rten mgon po 'dren par 'gyur//de yi smon lam de dag gis//phyogs bcu 'di dag rgyal ba(sa)
8b7(1): gang de nas byang chub sems dpa' dri ma med pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad 
8b8(1): pa 'di dag smras so//ye shes dkyil 'khor sangs rgyas te//'jig rten chos rnams rnam par sbyong //'jig rten chos rnams snam sbyangs nas//sangs rgyas chos 
9a1(1): @#//rnams rab tu brjod//de ste sems can mnyam sangs rgyas//sems can la las bsam byas na//rgyal ba de dag thams cad mthong //sangs rgyas la ni 
9a2(1): gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas la ni dmigs byas nas//sems kyis sangs rgyas dmigs pas na//sems bskyed pa dang rgyal mnyam par//de bzhin de dag rgyal ba mthong //
9a3(1): dkar ba'i chos kyis nyer brtan zhing //gang gis grags pa rnam sbyangs pa//de dag thams cad sems bskyed la//shes rab ldan pa rab tu zhugs//dmigs pa dag dang byin 
9a4(1): gyi rlabs//mi yi gtso bo ston pa mdzad//de phyir dmigs pa kun du yang //sangs rgyas rgyal ba gshegs par brjod//'jig rten khams rnams 'di dag ni//sangs rgyas 
9a5(1): sku rnams bdag gi med//sems la bdag gi med pa bstan//byang chub rtogs la bdag gi med//mi yi seng ge'i chos nyid dang //dpa' ba'i stobs la rab tu gnas//
9a6(1): lus dang mtshan nyid mi 'dra ste//snang ba'i sems dang mnyam pa'i sku//ji ltar 'jig rten de bzhin sku//de bzhin skur yang rgyal ba ste//sku yi rang bzhin shes pa ni//sangs 
9a7(1): rgyas chags pa med ces bya//sangs rgyas kun tu gzigs pa ste//chos rnams thams cad rab tu gzigs//sangs chos dang byang chub rnams//gang langa' dmigs pa med 
9a8(1): pa yin//sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas gang na'ang mi bzhugs te//yang dag sangs rgyas log pa med//de bzhin gshegs la len mi mnga'//
9b1(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpal gyis rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par ltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras 
9b2(1): so sangs rgyas thams cad kun du khyab//zhing gcig la ni mi gnas te//thams cad du ni sangs rgyas gshegs//zhing rnams kun du rab tu snang //sangs rgyas sku ni tham(sa)
9b3(1): cad du byin gyis rlabs dag rab tu 'byung //'tshang rgya chos kyi 'khor lo dang //bsam pa bzhin du mya ngan 'da'//sangs rgyas la ni su zhig sems//ci phyir sangs rgyas 
9b4(1): bsam mi khyab//yang dag sangs rgyas su yis mthong //rgyal ba sus kyang mthong bar 'gyur//ji ltar chos dang chos nyid dang //ji lta sangs rgyas spyod yul du //gang dag chos   
9b5(1): rnams gcig tsam yang //de bzhi(na) nyid la mi 'jug go//'di ni 'jig rten 'di rgyal zhes//'jig rten dag na rnam rtog yod //gang gis ngo bo nyid shes na //'jig rten med de rgyal ba 'ang med //'jig rten 
9b6(1): dad par bya ba'i phyir //mgon po dag ni mngon sum sten //de dag gi ni blta ba 'di //shin tu dmigs par mi rung ngo //gang gis chags pa rnam ldog cing //chags pa med la dga' byed pa//

9b8(1): cad du /gang gis chos nyid 'di shes shing //gang gis 'di bzhin yid shes na//thams cad mkhyen cing kun gzigs pa'i//de bzhin gshegs par myur du 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams 
11a1(1): @#//de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya stong phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed thams cad kyang mtshan mtshan mthun pa sha stag gis 'di ltar bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang 
11a2(1): mdzad 'di'i sngon gyi smon lam dang /sngon gyi byin gyis rlabs dang /khyod kyis ye shes rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa bas bstan pa'i phyir 'di lta ste/byang 
11a3(1): chub sems dpa'i mi 'jigs pa rnam par dag par bya ba dang /spobs pa chags pa med par bya ba dang /ye shes kyis bsgribs pa med pa'i sar 'jug par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye 
11a4(1): shes yangs pa la rab tu 'jug par bya ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba mi zad pa phun sum tshogs par bya ba dang /bsgribs pa med pa'i chos dkar po yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings la sgo kun nas 'jug 
11a5(1): par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi cho 'phral bstan par bya ba dang /sngon gyi mtha'i mur thug pa'i ye shes dang /dran pa rgyun mi gcod par rnam par dag par bya ba dang /dbang po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byin   
11a6(1): gyi brlabs par bya ba dang /chos gyi sgo mtha' yas ba bstan par bya ba dang /shes pa kun bzung bar bya (ba)dang /'di lta ste yongs su bsngo ba bcu yongs su rdzogs par bstan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams 
11a7(1): cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba gzung bar bya ba dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i tshogs rnams rab tu bstan par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mi(ma)chod par bya ba dang /smon lam chen po'i skabs dbye bar bya 
11a8(1): ba dang ;don shin du bstan pa/la 'jug par bya ba dang /chos kyis dbyings yongs su shes par bya ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi yid rab tu dga' bar bya dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi srid pa   
11b1(1): gsum gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba 'thun pa la brten par bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs yang dag par gzung bar bya ba'i phyir byin gyis brlab pa mdzad kyi /kye rigs kyi bu khyod kyis  
11b2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa'i mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i bsod rnams kyis brtan pa dang /gzungs kyi snang ba la zhugs pa dang /sangs 
11b3(1): rgyas kyi chos bsgribs pa med pa la zhugs pa dang /chos kyi dbyings snang ba'i chos nyid du bya ba dang /kha nas ma tho ba med pa'i chos dkar po yang dag par bsgrub par bya ba dang /ye shes 
11b4(1): kyi spyod yul yangs pa la gnas pa dang //bsgribs pa med pa'i chos snang ba la gnas pas chos bstan pa 'di brjod pa dang /bstan pa'i phyir spobs par gyis shig /de nas sangs  
11b5(1): rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi ye shes tshad med pa'i nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//spobs pa chags pa med pa dang /tshig rab tu dbye  
11b6(1): ba la mkhas pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do //bsgrib pa med pa'i chos kyi snang ba nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku dang gcig pa nye bar bsgrub pa  
11b7(1): mdzad do//chad med pa'i dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad( )do//byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnam par dbye ba la  
11b8(1): mkhas pas nye bar bsgrubs( ) mdzad do//de dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad nyams pa med par yongs su bsngos pa'i ye shes nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do //chos thams cad kyi lhag    
12a1(1): @#//mthong mngon par bskyed pa la mkhas pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//chos thams cad bstan pa'i gnas rgyun mi chod pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad//
12a2(1): de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i gzi brjid kyi phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag phyag g.yas pas/byang 
12a3(1): chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi spyi bo la byug pa mdzad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje rgyal mtshan la reg ma thag 
12a4(1): tu ting nge 'dzin de las rnam par langs te//langs nas kyang byang chub sems dpa' de dag la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam 
12a5(1): 'di lta ste/'gro ba thams cad khyab par byed pa'i sems can thams cad yongasu skyob pa 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dgade la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' sems 
12a6(1): dpa' chen po rnams 'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyis yongs su sngo ba la slob bo//ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba la slob

12a8(1): bsngo ba gang la bsams te/de skad du smras pa de dag ni gang /kye rgyal ba'i sras po dag yongs su bsngo ba'i grangs ni du zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dgab sngo ba bcu po 'di dag ni 'das pa dang 
12b1(1): ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sems can rnams kyi 'du shes    
12b2(1): 'gog cing 'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyob pa zhes bya ba byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba dang po dang /mi phyed pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba byang chub se mas dpa' rnams kyi yong+sa 
12b3(1): su bsngo ba gnyis pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad la yang dag par bskyed pa zhes bya ba byang chub kyi sems dpa' rnams kyis yongs su bsngo ba gsum pa dang //thams cad du rjes su song ba zhes bya  
12b4(1): ba byang chub sems dpa' rna mas kyis yongs su bsngo bzhi pa dang /bsod nams kyi mdzod mi zad pa zhes bya ba byang chub sesams dpa' rnams kyi yongasasu bsngo ba lnga pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba 
12b5(1): thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid kyis rjes su song ba zhes bya ba byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba drug pa dang /'gro ba thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyis rjes gsu song ba zhes bya ba//
12b6(1): byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi yongs su bsngo ba bdun pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin zhes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi yongs su bsngo ba brgyad 
12b7(1): pa dang chags pa med cing bcings pa dang dkrol ba med pa zhes bya ba byang chubasems dpa' rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba dgu pa dang //chos kyi dbyings tshad med pa'i rjes su song ba zhes bya 
12b8(1): ba byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyis yongs su bsngo ba bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis yongs su bsngo pa bcu po de dag ni 'das pa dang //

25a1(1): @#/ O /yul 'di //shin tu dog pa'i 'pho rtson rar sdug bsngal sems can rnams nad dang rga bas gzir cing tshan gyis mnar ba dag/mthong nas bdag gis thabs kyi chos rnams kun brtsams 
25a2(1): te/sems can kun sgrol sems dpa' de dag spyod yul 'di//sangs rgyas chos rnams thams cad thos 'gyur the tsom med /chos dbyings spyod yul phyogs bcu kun tu rgyas bkang ste//'di dag 
25a3(1): thams cad brtags na gsog kyang mi 'jigs shing //sems can la snyoms byed de'i spyod yul lo//de nas 'od zer de dag 'jig rten gyi khams de dag nas 'das te/shar phyogs logs su 'jig 
25a4(1): rten gyi khams bye ba (dpe )stong snang (ba)byas so/'jig rten gyi khams bye ba rgya phrag stong snang bar byas so//'jig rten gyi khams bye ba khrag khrig brgya snang bar byas so//'jigrten gyi khams khrag khrig stong 
25a5(1): snang bar byas so//'jigarten gyi khams bye ba khrag khri brgya phrag stong snang bar byas so//'jig rten gyi khams tshad med pa snang bar byas so//'jigs rten gyi khams gzhal du med pa snang bar byas so/
25a6(1): 'jig rten gyi khams grangs med pa snang bar byas so //de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams bsam gyi mi khyab pa/mtshungs pa med pa/kun tu mtha' yas pa mu med ba/sa mtshams med pa/dpag 
25a7(1): med pa/brjod du med pa dag kyang snang bar byas so//'ji rten gyi khams (mas)kyi dbyings kyis klas (pa)//nam mkha'i mtha' gtugs pa thams cad snang bar byas so//bzhung de bzhin du (lho) phyogs dang /
25a8(1): de bzhin du nub phyogs dang/de bzhin du byang phyogs dang/de bzhin du(    ) byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams dang/de bzhin du shar lho'i phyogs mtshams dang/de bzhin du lho nub kyi phyogs mtshams 
25b1(1): dang /de bzhin du nub byang gi phyogs mtshams dang //de bzhin du 'og gi phyogs dang /steng gi phyogs kyi bar du 'jig rten gyi khams bye ba snang bar byas so//'jig rten gyi khams bye brgya snang bar byas
25b2(1): so//'jig rten gyi khams bya ba stong snang bar byas nas/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pa'i bar gyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyang snang bar byas so/de ltar phyogs bcur shin tu rgyas par byas
25b3(1): nas/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyang snang bar bar byas so//'jig rtenagyi khams re rer yang 'dzam bu'i gling bye ba phrag brgya bstan pa nas/gzhan de bzhin du 'og min bye ba phrag brgya'i
25b4(1): bar du bstan to//'jig rten gyi khams de dag gi khongs su gang ji yang rung ste gtogs par gyur pa de dag thams cad kyang ril kyis bstan to//ji ltar bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas kyi gdan la 
25b5(1): bzhugs shing //byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig par snang ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams re re'i gling bzhi ba bye ba phrag brgya kun na yang /de bzhin
25b6(1): gshegs pa bye ba phrag brgya dag sangs rgyas kyi gdan la bzhugs par snang ngo//thams cad du yang sangs rgyas kyi byin kyi rlabs kyis bcu nas/byang chub sems dpa' bcu bcu 'ongs par gyur 
25b7(1): to//byang chub sems dpa'ade dag thams cad kyang ming 'di zhes bya ba dag ste//'di ltar 'dzam dpal zhes bya ba nas/bzang po'i dpal gyi bar dag go//byang chub sems dpa'adi dag thams
25b8(1): cad kyang 'jig rten gyi khams ming 'di zhes bya ba dag nas 'ongs pa dag ste/'di ltar gser gyi mdog ces bya ba nas/'dra ba'i mdog ces bya ba'i bar dag go//byang chub sems dpa' de
26a1(1): @#/ O /dag thams cad kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan 'di zhes bya ba dag gi zham ring na tshangs pa'i sbyang pa sbyong ba dag ste/'di ltar rig byed mi g.yo ba zhes bya ba nas/rig byed sna 
26a2(1): tshogs zhes bya ba'i bar dag go//byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad kyang /byang chub sems dpa' re re zhing/byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang lhan cig tu 
26a3(1): byin ba dag ste/gcig ji lta ba de bzhin thams cad do/thams cad na yang byang chub sems 'jam dpal zhes bya ba sha stag don 'di nyid rjod do//gcig na ji lta ba de bzhin thams cad na yang rjod do/de'i tshe 
26a4(1): tshogs su bcad pa 'di dag kyang so so nas sma bar gyur ro//bskal pa mtha' yas bsam gyis mi khyab sems kyi skad cig la//gang gis mthong yang bsams med 'gro dang    dngos po'i tshul med pa'o//
26a5(1): lam du rab tu 'jug pa de ni thams cad shes 'gyur te//stobs bcus tshul 'di ji ltar mkhyen bzhin 'jigs med sangs rgyas 'gyur//grags pa snyan par phyogs rnams kun tu mtshungs med grags 
26a6(1): gyur cig//bgegs rnams kun las 'das te chos nyid 'di la dgar sbyad de//tshad med thams cad so sor zhi zhing don rnams kun rtogs pas//de phyir phyogs rnams kun bkang bde gshegs chos nyid 
26a7(1): 'di ston to//dgongs pa ji bzhin de ni spyod pa'i 'bras bu 'thun pa ste//'jig rten don 'gyur de bzhin gshegs la mchod pa yangs byas shing//de dag thams cad so sor 'thun par ye shes rnam 
26a8(1): bsgoms pas//sems can dam pa sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul 'di 'drar phyogs bcur ston//thog mar nyan 'dod bla ma bsnyen bkun bzod (pa)des byas shing//rang bzhin med pa'i chos la rab rtog bsam 
26b1(1): gtan bsnyen byed pa//bde gshegs nyid ni rtogs 'gyur 'gro ba rnam sgom sems tshim byed//chos nyid de 'dra spyod pa myur du bder gshegs thob par 'gyur/snying nas 'dri zhing phyogs rnams rgyas 
26b2(1): byed rtse gcig thung byed cing //rgyal ba'i yon dan mos las mi zlog spyod pas tshim byed pa//dngos dang dngos med thams cad shes byas spangs shing dngos zad pa'i//sangs rgyas bdag med dpe med 
26b3(1): mthong ba de dag lus 'di yin//bzang po'i spyod yul can gyi sems kyis phyogs rnams kun bkang zhing/zhing rnams thams cad chos kyi gter gyis khyab byas tshim byed pa//ji bzhin chos nyid mtha' 
26b4(1): la the tsom med pa'i rnams//stobs bcu ji 'dra de bzhin 'gyur ba'i lus ni 'di dag go//bcom ldan 'das kyis chos gang sga brjod de la 'jug byed cing//chos kyi 'khor lo ji bzhin blta dka' gzhal 
26b5(1): yas rtag skor ba//de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i gsang kun byang chub yan lag rtogs//de ltar 'jug pas rtag tu sangs rgyas sku rab 'di dag mthong /mthong yang mig gi rnam shes 
26b6(1): tshul du rdul med mthong ba med//rnam shes sgyu ma bzhin pas mig stong stobs bcu mi mthong ngo//'du shes thams cad rnam rtog yin gyi sangs rgyas mthong ba min//shin tu chags med yul spyod de 
26b7(1): ni rgyal ba mthong ba yin//sems can las mang bsags pa de dag shin tu blta dka' ste//nang dang phyi dang byibs dang mdog tu phyogs bcu kun med//de bzhin rang bzhin gzhal yas phyogs 
26b8(1): rnams kun 'gebs pa//shes rab dka' ba 'dren pa chen po bsgribs pa med pas brnyes//nam mkha' dag la zhing rnams byed ba dngos por gnas par snang//de nas 'ong ba med cing phyogs bcu 
27a1(1): @#/ O /kun tu 'gro ba med//tshig cing gnas bar gyur kyang de dag ci la'ang gnas ba nas/de bzhin rang 'byung rnams kyi sku ni mkha' dbyings rtag tu 'gengs//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo/sangs
27a2(1): rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od zer las rnam par sangs rgyas pa'i le'u ste////bcu bzhi ba'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal gyis//byang chub sems dba'
27a3(1): sangs rgyas dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras rang bzhin gcig pa'i sems la//bye brag rnam pa tha dad pa 'di lta bu dag 'di lta ste/bde 'gror 'gro ba dang/ngan song du 'gro ba dang/
27a4(1): dbang po tshang ba dang/dbang po ma tshang ba dang/skye ba 'thun pa dang/skye ba mi 'thun pa dang/sdug pa dang/mi sdug pa dang/bde ba myong ba dang/sdug bsngal myong ba snang ba dag la ltos/las kyis sems
27a5(1): mi shes so/sems kyis ni las mi shes so/tshor bas ni rnam par smin pa mi shes so/rnam bar smin pas ni tshor ba mi shes so/sems kyis ni tshor ba mi shes so//tshor bas ni sems mi shes so/rgyus
27a6(1): ni rkyen mi shes so/rkyen kyis ni rgyu mi shes so/dngos pos ni dmigs pa mi shes so//dmigs pas ni dngos po mi shes so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpA
27a7(1): 'jam dpal la tshigasu bcad de smras pa/sems can bstan par bya ba'i phyir//gang 'di yongs su dris pa dag/chos rnams chos nyid ji 'dra ba//'jam dpal bdag las mnyan bar gyis/chos rnam
27a8(1): 'di dag g.yo ba med/shin tu di ngos po med pa ste/de phyir gzhan gyis gzhan dag gi//ngo bo nyid kyang mi shes so//ji ltar chu klung rgyud kyi rnams//gzhan dang gzhan du 'grogs rgyu yang//gcig ni gcig 
27b1(1): gis mi shes te//chos kyi chos nyid de bzhin no/ji ltar 'bar ba'i mtshan ma can//'od 'gro gzhan dang gzhan 'grogs kyang/gcig gis gcig ni mi shes te//chos kyi chosanyid de bzhin no/ji ltar
27b2(1): rlung ni sten tshul gyis//gzhan dang gzhan gyis rab tu rgyu//gcig gis gcig ni mi shes/chos kyi chos nyid de bzhin ni//ji ltar sa'i khams kyi rnams//gcig ni gcig la gnas gyur kyang/gcig
27b3(1): gis gcig ni med shes te//chos kyi chos ny-id de bzhin no//yi ge dang rna ba sna dag dang//lce dang lus dang yid kyi rnams//'di dag las ni rgyu 'god kyang//byed pa po ni gang yang med//'byung ba'i
27b4(1): rang bzhin med ba yis//'byung ba nye bar bstan byas kyang//bstan par byas pa gang yang med//bstan pa la ni dngos medo//mig ni rang bzhin nyid kyis stong/rna ba sna yang rang bzhin gyis/lce dang
27b5(1): lus dang yid rnams kyang//med de yongs su brtags pa yin//gang zhig rang bzhin dngos med par//tshul bzhin du ni rtags byed pa//de'i mig la 'khrul ba med//dngos mig bsam gyis mi
27b6(1): khyab po//yang dag pa yang yang dag min//bden nam 'on te brdzun yang rung//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten kun/tshig gi lam gyis brjod pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal
27b7(1): gyis/byang chub sems dpa' nor dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//rgyal ba'i sras/sems can thams cad sems can med mong gyi//'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can 'dul ba'i
27b8(1): dus la ji lta ba dbab pa dang/sems can gyi tshe ji lta ba dang/lus ji lta ba dang/spyod pa ji lta ba dang/mos pa ji lta ba dang/mtshan ji lta ba dang/bsam pa ji lta ba dang/sbyor ba ji lta ba dang/sems
28a1(1): @#/ O /ji lta ba dang/spyod pa ji lta ba bzhin du/sems can rnams kyis sems can gdul ba'i phyir mthong ngo/de nas byang chub sems dpa' nor dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' 
28a2(1): 'jam dpal la tshigs su bcad de smras pa//gang rnams rab tu zhi spyod pa/thos dang ldan pa'i sa 'di yin//'di don ci 'dra de bzhin du//'jam dpal bdag las mnyan par gyis/bdag 
28a3(1): gi lus la su gnas shes/gang gis nang gi rab shes pa/yang dag yod dam med pa'i don//des ni rab tu shes pa'i don//des ni rab du shes pa yin//lus kyi rnam bar dgod pa ni/phyogs na mi gnas brjod
28a4(1): ba yin//de bas shes pa de gnas la//nam yang tsha chags par mi byed do//lus ni rnam bar spyod pa yi//dngos po kun kyang rnam par bsgoms//sems ni phyir ldog mi 'gyur te//dngos po kun ni brtags 
28a5(1): pa'o//gang gi tshe rjes su 'brang //gang gi tshe ni phyir ldog 'gyur//ji ltar mgal med 'khor lo yi//thog ma tha ma 'khor ba bzhin//dngos po thams cad mi brtag par//skye bo gang rnams rtog pa'ang/lus 
28a6(1): la bdag kyang med ba'i phyir//'du shes phyir yang ldog par 'gyur//bdag med rmi lam lta bu yi//las ni kun tu rab tu 'byung //rnam bar smin pas rab tu 'byung //phyi ma ci bzhin sngon de bzhin//sems ni
28a7(1): dbang byed yin par bshad//dad ba'i tshul ni ji bzhin du//tshul bzhin ma yin ma phyin ci log 'jig rten thams cad ston pas rab tu 'byung//ji snyed 'jig rten brda yod pa//de dag thams cad brtags pa ste/
28a8(1): chos nyid de nam du yang //chos gcig la yang mi gnas so//rkyen rnams la ni rten bcas te//chos rnams so sor rab tu 'byung/de dag ldog par de bzhin 'gyur/phyi ma ji bzhin sngon de bzhin//de nas byang chub 
28b1(1): sems dpa' 'jam dpal gyis//byang chub sems dpa' chen cen dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras sems can thams cad kyi 'byung ba chen po bzhi rnams yongs su 'dzin pa med cing 
28b2(1): bdag gi med la mnyam ba yin yang /'on kyang bde ba'i tshor ba dag kyang snang//sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba dag kyang snang/yid du 'ong bar byed pa dang/yid du mi 'ong bar byed pa dang/nang du byed pa dang/phyi rol 
28b3(1): du byed pa dang/tshor ba che ba dang/tshor ba chung ba dang/tshe 'di la myong ba dang/tshe gzhan la myong ba dag kyang snang la/chos kyi dbyings la ni/yid du 'ong ba 'am/med pa 'di ci zhig de nas byang chub sems 
28b4(1): dpa' rin cen dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' 'jal dpal la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/ji ltar las rnams byas 'gyur pa/tshor ba de bzhin 'byung 'gyur gyi/'di la byed pa gang yang med 
28b5(1): 'di ni sangs rgyas bstan pa'o/shin tu phyis pa'i me long la/de ni ji ltar 'dug pa bzhi ni/de la de ni de bzhin snang /chos kyi rang bzhin de yin no/ji ltar zhing dang sa bon dag/gcig gyis gcig 
28b6(1): ni mi shes kyang/rang bzhin nye bar ston pa byed /las kyi rang bzhin de bzhin no/sgyu ma byed ba mkhas pa zhig/lam po cher ni song 'dug ste//gzugs rnams ston par byed pa bzhin//las kyi rang 
28b7(1): bzhin de bzhin no//ji ltar shing gi 'khrul 'khor rnams//sna tshogs sgra mang brjod byed kyang/de ni bdag dang bdag med min//las kyi rang bzhin de bzhinano/ji ltar bya rnams mang po yi//sgo nga'i 
28b8(1): sbubs ni phye ba las//sgra skang gzhan dang gzhan 'gyur ba//las kyi rang bzhin de bzhin no/ji ltar ma yi mngal du yang//bu ni 'pho bar mi 'gyur te//'on kyang dbang po sna tshogs 'gyur //las kyi rang 
29a1(1): @#/ O /bzhin de bzhin no/ji ltar sems dmyal 'jigs pa na/gshin rje'i rigs gtogs sdug bsngal ba/de dag la ni 'ong ba med//las kyi rang bzhin de bzhin no//ji ltar rin 
29a2(1): cen mchog rnams dang/lha'i bu rnams kun ldan yang//'pho par snang ba gang yang med//de bzhin las kyi rang bzhin no//ji ltar 'jig rten la la dag//bsam gyis mi khyab 'bar 'gyur 
29a3(1): kyang//de dag 'ong ba gang yang med//las kyi rang bzhinade bzhin no//de nas byang chub sems 'jam dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal 
29a4(1): ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs pas chos gcig mngon bar sangs rgyas na/'on kyang chos tshad med (par)yang/yang dag par ston zhing tshad med par yang rnam par ston/sems can tshad med yang 
29a5(1): rab tu ston /gsung tshad ba yang shin tu sgrog/sku tshad med ba yang shin tu ston/bsam batshad med pa yang thugs su chud/rdzu 'phrul tshad med ba yang yang dag par ston/'jig rten gyi khams tshad 
29a6(1): med pa yang shin tu skyed/rgyun tshad med pa yang byin kyis rlob/sbyod yul tshad med pa yang shin tu bstan la/chos kyi dbyings kyang rnam pa tha dad du mi snang ba 'di ji zhig/de nas byang chub sems
29a7(1): dba' yon tan dpal gyis/byang chub sem dpa' 'jam dpal la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/gnas 'di shes par dka' ba ste//rgyal ba'i sras kyis bdag la dris//de ni mkhas pas rnam 
29a8(1): par shes//yon tan mos pa dpyod par byed//ji ltar sa ni gcig pu yang//sems can mang por rab tu rgyu//de la gcig dang tha dad med //sangs rgyas chos nyid de bzhin no//ji ltar me ni gcig bu yang/
29b1(1): 'jig rten kun na kun kyang 'bud//de la me lce tha dad med//sangs rgyas chos nyid de bzhin no//ji ltar rgya mtsho gcig pu la//chu klung tshang med 'bab gyur kyang//de la chu'i bye brag med/sangs rgyas
29b2(1): chos nyid de bzhin no//jiltar rlung ni gcig pu yang//'jig rten kun tu rgyu zhing (de) lang//de la gcig dang tha dad med/sangs rgyas chos nyid de bzhino ni//sprin chen rnams ni 'brug bsgags shing/
29b3(1): sa rnams kun kyang ngoms par byed//de'i char la bye brag med//sangs rgyas chos (nyid) de bzhin no//ji ltar sa ni gcig pu yang//myu gu sna tshogs 'byin cing ston/sa la bye brag 'byed pa med//sangs 
29b4(1): rgyas chos nyid de bzhin de bzhin no//ji ltar bsgribs med nyi ma ni//phyogs bcu kun tu snang bar byed//de la 'od zer tha dad med//sangs rgyasachos nyid de bzhin no//ji( )ltar nam mkha'i zla ba ni/
29b5(1): 'jig rten kun na rab tu snang//gnas nas 'gyur ba gang yang med//sangs rgyas chos nyid de bzhin no//ji ltar tshangs pa mi mjed bdag//stong gsum na ni 'dug mod kyi//de la lus ni bye 
29b6(1): brag med//sangs rgyas chosanyid de bzhin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' mig gi dpal la 'di skad smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin 
29b7(1): gshegs pa la dge ba skyed par gcig kyang de bzhin gshegs pa la dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed ba'i sems can rnams gzugs sna tshogs dang/   rigs sna tshogs dang/dbang po sna tshogs dang/
29b8(1): longs spyod sna tshogs dang/dbang sna tshogs dang/'khor sna tshogs dang/phyug dbul sna tshogs dang/yon tan sna tshogs dang/shes rab sna tshogs su yang snang la/de bzhin gshegs pa 
30a1(1): @#/ O /ni gang la yang mi mnyam pa 'am/tha dad du dgongs pa gang yang med na/'di ci zhig//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mig gi dpal gyis//byang chub sems dpa' 'jal 
30a2(1): dpal la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/ji ltar sa ni ro gcig kyang //sa bon tshul bzhin myu gu ston//byams dang sdang bar sems pa med//sangs rgyas yon phul de bzhin no//ji ltar chu 
30a3(1): ni ro gcig kyang//snod kyi dbang gis rnam par 'gyur //de bzhin sangs rgyas yon phul ba//rgyud (sku)rnams la rnam par 'gyur //ji ltar sgyu mkhan mkhas pa ni//rtag tu 'khor kyang mgu 
30a4(1): bar byed de bzhin sangs rgyas yon phu la ba//shin tu sbyangs pa dga' bar byed//ji ltar mkhas pa'i rgyal po dag//'gro ba mang po shes par byed//de bzhin sangs rgyas yin phul ba/
30a5(1): ji ltar bskyed bzhin shes par byed//ji ltar dri med mi long la//ci 'dra'i gzugs rnams de bzhin snang //de bzhin sems can bsam tshul du//sangs rgyas yon phul rnam dag 'gyur /
30a6(1): ji ltar dug phan sman gyi rnams//dug nad thams cad sel par byed//de bzhin sadas rgyas yon phul ba//nyon mongs dug ni chen po sel/ji ltar nyi ma shar ba na//'gro ba 'di dag snang 
30a7(1): bar byed //mun pa dag kyang rnam par sel//sangs rgyas yon phulade bzhin no//ji ltar zla ba dri med ba//kun tu mnyam pa nyid byas te//sa ni rab tu snang bar byed//sangs rgyas yon phul de bzhin
30a8(1): no//ji ltar chu ni stobs shugs kyis//sa ni thams cad g.yo 'gyur ba//sangs rgyas yon phul dai bzhin du//srid gsum 'gro ba rnams kyang skyong//'bar dang 'od 'phro bcas pa'i me/thams 
30b1(1): cad dag du sreg pa'i dngos de bzhin sangs rgyas yon phul ba//'dus byed thams cad sreg bar byed//de nas byang chub sems 'jam 'dal gyis//byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus
30b2(1): dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa gcig na//thams cad blta ba 'thun bas nyon mongs pa thams cad spang par ci'i phyir
30b3(1): mi 'gyur//de dag kun la yang gzugs nyid du zad//tshor ba 'ang tshor ba nyid du zad//'du shes kyang 'du shes nyid du zad//'du byed rnams kyang 'du byed rnams nyid du zad//rnam par shes
30b4(1): pa yang rnam par shes pa nyid du zad//'dod pa'i khams kyang 'dod pa'i khams nyid du zad//gzugs kyi khams kyang gzugs kyi khams nyid du zad//gzu+ugs med pa'i khams kyang
30b5(1): gzugs med pa'i khams nyidu zad//ma rigs pa yang ma rig pa nyid du zad//sred pa yang sred pa nyid du zad la/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ba'i bstan pa yang phan pa 'am gnod par
30b6(1): byed pa ma yin na de ci'i phyir//de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus dpal gyis//byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal la tshigs su bcad de smras pa//de ltar khyed kyis

61b5(1): 'di la de bzhin gshegs pa rang 'byung dpal//mtsho gsal dang bar yan lag bzhi'i dbung//so so rang (ra) gzugs brnyan tshul bzhin mthong //ral gyi shang lang sogs ka mde'u thung 
61b6(1): mnga'/zhon pa theg (pa)go cha bcas par mthong//gang gi gang gzugs cha byad ci 'dra ba//chu gsal de la de bzhin ma nor mthong//chu la zhe sdang byams pa rtog pa med//drang srong ting 'dzin 
61b7(1): rnam 'phrul de 'dra'o//rgya mtsho'i lha mo sgra dang ldan yod pa//sems cad rgya mtshor ji snyed skyes pa rnams/de dag sgra'i yan lag dbyangs tshul shes/sgra skad kun tu rang rang skad du brjod//
61b8(1): dbyangs ldan de ni 'dod chags zhe sdang cen//'on kyang sgra kun dbyangs kyi tshul la mkhas//gzungs dang chos kyi stobs la dbang thob rnams//'jig rten lhar bcas ci phyir mgur mi 
62a1(1): @#//byed//dga' byed chung ma sgra nyan ldan pa mo//tshangs pa'i bu mo sems las 'byung ba ste//dam tshig bden bsgrub mi 'tshe dpa' ba'i//sems yid dag rnams byin 
62a2(1): kyis rlob pa byed//sgra nyan ldan pa 'dod chags zhe sdang can//brtul zhugs grub la dbyangs kyis rjes su 'drang//'jig rten yid 'ong sgras kyang tshim bar byed/ye shes yon tan 
62a3(1): mnga' ba ci cir mi ston//sngags kyi tshul mkhas sgyu ma'i mkhan po yang//sna tshogs mtha' yas gzugs mang ston pa byed//nyin mtshan yun dang zla ba lo brgya dang//khyim bzad 
62a4(1): rgyas pa shin tu mdzes pa ston//'dod chags sdang ldan sgyu ma'i mkhan po yang/sgyu ma'i 'phrul gyis 'jig rten dga' bar byed//bsam gtan mngon shes rnam 'phrul rab byang rnam//

62a8(1): rnams kun la'ang mche ba drug drug ste//so rnams kun la yang re re rdzing gi rnams/bdun dang bdun bcus rgyas 'ongs sprul pa byed/rdzing rnams re re kun du pad ma las/bdun bdun 
62b1(1): rgyan kyis rnam brgyan sprul pa byed//pad ma re re la'i bu mo drug//sgyu rtsal kun mkhas bdun bdun sprul ba byed//de dag rtsed mo glu gar tshul la mkhas//sum bcu rtsa gsum dbang po 
62b2(1): de dang rtse//glang po de yang klu'i lus btang zhing //klu rnams kun dang mnyam zhing 'dra bar rdza//spyod pa'i tshul dang longs spyod kun dang 'thun//glang po mchog gi rnam 'phrul ji 'dra 
62b3(1): ltos//de ni 'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug can//'on kyang de 'dra'i rdzu 'phrul ston byed na//shes rab thabs kyi stobs la byang gyur rnams//ting 'dzin brgyas kyang ci rab 'phrul 
62b4(1): mi byed//sgra gcan ji dga'i tshul du lus rdo rje'i gzhi la rkang ba'i mthil bor zhing //rgya mtsho'i gzabs ring lde bar phyin tsam byed//ri rab rtse dang nyid kyi mgo yang snyom//de ni 'dod chag+sa

62b6(1): byin 'phrul la 'ang ltos shig dang //lha dang lha min 'thab bar gyur ba'i tshe//lha min byed ba 'phyes pa ji snyed pa//de snyed brgya sbyin rang gi lus 'phul byed//lha min dbang po lha min phal 
62b7(1): kun kyis//brgya byin rang rang mdun du 'dug par shes//de ni rdo rje 'chang bas bzung gyur ces//lha min dbang po kun kyang mgo mos so//'jigs pa'i gzugs kyi mig stong rab bstan 
62b8(1): te//rdo rje me lce 'bar ba rab bzas shing //lus (la)go bgos gzwi rjid thub dga' ba'i//brgya byin mthong nas lha min dbang po 'byer//de ni bsod nams phal gyi stobs dang mthus/lha 
63a1(1): @#/ O /rgyal don tu rnam par 'phrul byed na//'gro ba ma lus kun kyi mgon gyur ba'i //bsod nams mi zad rnams ko cir mi 'phrul//lha rnams bskul pa'i lha rdzas rnga yod 
63a2(1): ba//lha'i las rnams rnam par smin pas byung //lha rnams bag med sbyong pas shes gyur nas//mkhas las rnga'i dbyangs kyang rab tu sgrog//'dod (pa)thams cad mi rtag mi bstan

63a4(1): gyi lam yin 'chi med lam ma yin //ji snyed bag med rnam par spyod pa'i rnams//bdud kyi chu srin chen pos zos ba yin//yongs su 'dzin pa thams cad sdug bsngal rtsa//skye ba

63b1(1): med stobs dang shin tu ldan par 'gyur//lha min dbang po 'jigs shing dngangs gyur na//de ma thag tu 'jigs shing kun tu 'byer//de bzhin rnga 'dra 'chi med stobs pa'i rnams/

63b7(1): 'gro ba mang po bdud kyi dbang byed na//dbang bcu'i dbang thob gyur pa ji ro ste//'gro ba thams cad rang gi phyogs mi gzhog//stod gsum bdag po tshangs pa chen po yang//stong gsum
63b8(1): kun kyi tshangs pa'i gnas rnams na//ma lus kun tu 'dug par rab tu snang//tshangs pa'i sgra dbyangs yid 'o rnams kyang sgro//de ni 'jig rten pa'i tshangs lam gyi/bsam
64a1(1): @#/ O /gtan mngon par shes pas rnam par 'khru la//'jig rten yongs 'das bla na med pa'i //bsam gtan rnam thar gnas pa cir mi 'phrul//dbang phyug chen  
64a2(1): chen po'i ye shes rnam 'phrul ltos//rgya mtsho'i sbyin kyis char rnam rab bkye ba//skad cig gcig gis thams cad bgrang ba byed //thigs 'ga'i zer kyang ma lus de shes 
64a3(1): na//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab tshul bslabs te//sangs rgyas ye shes bla na med thob rnams//ci rob 'gro ba ma lus sems can kyi//skad cig gcig la sems 
64a4(1): bsags shes mi 'gyur//sems can las smin bsam gyis mi khyab pas//rlung las 'jig (pa)shams cad yongs su byung//rgya mtsho ri dang lha'i gzhal med khang//sa bon rnams

64b1(1): byed//rgya mtsho 'di la ngo mtshar rmad kyi chos//'gro ba thams cad rgya dang mnyam snang zhing //chu dang dang 'byung po rin cen yongs mi zad//chu rnams bzung bas yongs su ngoms

64b8(1): gnas su gser gyi sprin bstan nas//nyi ma'i mdog 'dra glog kyang rab tu 'gyed//dga' ldan gnas su zla 'od bzang 'dra dang//dga' ldan gnas su 'dzam bu'i gser mdog ston/
65a1(1): @#//kha ba ba mo'i mdog tu mtshe mar te//sum bcu rtsa gsum gnas su gser mdog tu//rgyal (chen)gnas su dpal gyi snying po'i mdog//mu tig (ma)mar po'i mdog tu rgya mtshor

66a1(1): @#//klu'i 'phrul yang de ltar bsam mi khyab//de ltar chu'i rgya mtsho nas ba yang //klu'i rnam 'phrul bsam yas byed na//chos kyi rgya mtshor zhugs pa'i yon tan bdag//mtha'

66b8(1): //bam po nyi shu pa'o////de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus shar phyogs kyi 'jigs rten gyi khams thams cad dang//de bzhin du lho phyogs dang//nub phyogs dang//byang shar gyi phyogs 
67a1(1): @#/ O /mtshams dang//shar lho'i phyogs mtshams dang//lho nub kyi phyogs mtshams dang//nub byang gi phyogs mtshams dang//'og gi phyogs dang//steng gi phyogs gyis 'jig

67b1(1): ba//gos stong phrag bcus mngon par bkab pa//lha'i bu stong phrag bcus mdun du byas pa//tshangs pa stong phrag bcus kun nas bskor ba//'od zer stong phrag bcus shin 
67b2(1): tu snang bar byas//bcom ldan 'das kyi gdan du bshams so//ji ltar gling bzhi pa 'di'i ri rab kyi rtse mor lha'i dbang po'i khang pa rnam par rgyal bar (lha'i)dbang bo brgya byin gyis de

100b6(1): pa tha dad pa la mkhas pa mngon par 'tshang rgya bar ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnam bla na med pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sims skye'o//'di lta ste sems  
100b7(1): can gcig sems can grangs med par yang dag par 'jug par bya ba'i phyir/dad pa'i tshul brjod pa'i snang ba'i 'od kyis mos pa/gcig nas gcig du brgyud pa ma lus pa dang//
100b8(1): 'gro ba brgya mtsho mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bye ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//'di lta ste 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang /
101a1(1): @#//da ltar byung ba'i dge ba dang mi dge ba la mos pa tshad med pa la 'jug par bya ba dang/dus gsum thams cad kyi mos pa sna tshogs kyi dbang gis yongs 
101a2(1): su shes par bya ba dang/mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du mngon par 'tshang brgya bar bya ba'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//
101a3(1): 'di ltar ste skal ba mnyam pa'i mos pa dang/skal ba mi mnyam pa'i mos pa shes pa la 'jug par bya ba dang/mos pa thams cad mos pa gcig tu yang dag par 'jug pa dang//
101a4(1): mos pa gcig mos pa thams cad dang/mnyam pa nyid du mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba dang/de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs la rab tu 'jug pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpA
101a5(1): rnams bla na med pa yang dge bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//'di lta ste mos pa bla na ma dang bcas pa dang/mos pa bla na med pa dang/lhag ma dang bcas pa dang/lhag ma 
101a6(1): med pa dang/mnyam pa dang mi mnyam pa'i mos pa'i bye brag shes pa/mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i
101a7(1): byang chub tu sems skye'o//'di lta ste gnas pa'i mos pa dang/mi gnas pa'i mos pa dang /thun mong gi mos pa dang/thun mong ma yin pa'i mos pa dang/'dres pa dang/ma 'dres 
101a8(1): pa dang|dge ba dang/mi dge ba dang/'jig rten pa dang/'jig rten las 'das pa'i mos pa dang/chos thams cad kyi bye brag shin du shes pa'i dam pa'i mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i 
101b1(1): phyir|byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//'di lta ste dge ba'i mos (ba)dang/yangs pa'i mos pa dang/mos pa bar ma dang/tha 
101b2(1): dad pa dang/nges pa'i mos pa ma lus pa thams cad bsgribs pa med pa'i ye shes dang/chags pa med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pas mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i phyir||
101b3(1): byang chub sems dpa' rnam bla na med pa yang dag pa rjes rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu ses skye'o//'di lta ste rnam par dag pa'i mos pa dang/kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mos pa dang/yangs 
101b4(1): pa'i mos pa dang/dog pa'i mos pa dang/phra ba'i mos pa dang/rgya che ba'i mos pa dang/sems can gyis khams ma lus pa thams cad kyis mos pa'i tshul rnam pa tha dad pa la rab tu 'jug par 
101b5(1): bya ba dang/sems can gcig gi mos pa sna tshogs 'byam klas pa bcu mngon par 'tshang rgya bar ba bya ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
101b6(1): tu sems skye'o//'di lta ste rgyud stug mo'i mos pa dang sbyor ba'i mos pa dang/yongs su brtag pa'i mos pa dang/rang gis rjes su 'brang ba'i mos (ba)dang/rgyu'i tshogs dang ldan pa'i mos 
101b7(1): pa dang|rkyen gyi tshogs las byung ba'i mos ba dang/mos pa'i dra ba ma lus pa thams cad mnyam pa nyid du 'jug cing yongs su rig pa'i shes pas mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i phyir/byang 
101b8(1): chub sems dpa' bla na med pa yang bdag par rdzogs pa'i byang chu ba tu sems skye'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras mos pa sna tshogs kyi dpe tha dad pa snga ma de dag lta zhog gi/kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la skyes 
102a1(1): @#//bu la la zhig skyes par gyur te/de'i sems gcig bskyed pas/'jig rten gyi khams grangs med par gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi dbang po sna tshogs rab 
102a2(1): tu shes par gyur la/de ltar bu'i tshul du sems bskyed pa re re la shes pas/shar phyogs logs su bskal pa grangs med par zad par byas pa kyang/shar phyogs kyi sems can rnams kyi dbad po sna 
102a3(1): tshogs gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa'i mthar thug par ma rung la/tshul de bzhin du mos pa la/ji skad du brjod pa bzhin dbang po tha dad pa la yang shes par bya ste/de dag gi dbang po sna tshogs   
102a4(1): gcig nas gcig tu brgyud ba ni/phyogs bcur mthar thug par rung yang srid kyi /byang chub sems dang 'abla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems dang po bskyed pa'i dge ba'i 
102a5(1): rtsa ba ni mtha' med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag sems can de dag gi dbang po sna tshog shes par bya ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa  
102a6(1): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byad chub tu sems skye ba ma yin gyi/'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi dbang po yongs su shes par bya bA 
102a7(1): don du byang chub sems dpa' rnam bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byad chub tu ses skye'o//gzhung de bzhin du ji ltar mos pa la brjod pa de bzhin/dbang po rnams la yang 
102a8(1): shes par bya ste/thibs por gyur ba'i dbang po dang/sbyor ba dang ldan pa'i dbang po dang/yongs su brtags pa'i dbang po dang/ngag gi rjes su 'brang ba'i dbang po dang/rgyu'i tshogs 
102b1(1): dang ldan pa'i dbang po dang/rkyen gyi tshogs las byung ba'i dbang po dang/dbang po'i dra ba thams cad ma lus pa mnyam pa nyid du 'jug cing yongs su rig pa'i shes pas mngon par 'tshang rgya bar 
102b2(1): bya ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dbang po sna tshogs kyi dpe snga ma de dag lta zhog gi/kye rgyal 
102b3(1): ba'i sras dag 'di la skyes bu la la zhig skyes par gyur te/de'i sems bskyed pa gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams grangs med par gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi bsam pa sna tshogs   
102b4(1): yongs su shes par gyur la/tshul de lta bur sems bskyed pa re rer shes pas shar phyogs logs su bskal pa grangs med par zad par byas kyang/sems can gyi bsam pa sna tshogs kyi 
102b5(1): mthar phyin par ma rung la/gzhung de bzhin du phyogs bcu'i sems can rnams kyi bas pa sna tshogs ni shes par rung yang srid kyi/byang chub sems dpa' bla na med pa yang dag pa yang dag par 
102b6(1): rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sem sa dang po bskyed pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba ni mthar thug par mi rung ngo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras sems can de tsam dag gi bsam pa sna tshogs yongs su 
102b7(1): shes par bya ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems mi skye'i/'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du gtogs pa'i 
102b8(1): sems can rnams kyi bsam pa sna tshogs yongs du shes pas mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnam bla na med pa yang dag pas rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu se mas skyeste༐༐
103a1(1): @#//gzhung de ltar mos pa la ji ltar brjod pa bzhin du ste/bsam pa thibs por gyur pa dang/sbyor ba dang ldan pa'i bsam pa dang/yongs su brtags pa'i bsam 
103a2(1): pa dang|ngag gis rjes su 'brang ba'i bsam pa dang/rgyu'i tshogs dang ldan pa'i bsam pa dad/rkyen gyi tshogs las byung bsam pa'i bar du bsam pa'i dra ba ma lus pa thams cad mnyam pa

103b7(1): chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/sems can gyi khams kyis gtugs pa nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i sems can gyi khams mtha' dang /dbus med pa'i sems sna tshogs thams cad 
103b8(1): yongs su shes pas mngon par 'tshang brgya bar bya ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye'o//gzhung de ltar mos pa la ji skad du brjod
104a1(1): @#//pa bzhin te/sems can bskyed pa'i dra ba ma lus pa thams cad mnyam pa nyid du 'jug cing yongs su rig pa'i shes pas mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba'i phyir byang chub

105b7(1): 'og tu sim skyes bu gnyis pa zhig skyes zhes bya ba nas/skyes bu bcu pa skyes pa'i bar du ste/skyes bu dgu po de dag gis bskal pa grangs med pas nyon mongs pa sna tshogs rab tu she(sa)
105b8(1): pa de dag sems bskyed pa gcig gis yongs su shes par gyur la/tshul de'adra bas sems bskyed re rer yongs su shes pas /gzhung de bzhin du ste/phyogs bcu'i nyon mongs pa sna tshog+sa
106a1(1): @#//gcig nas gcig tu brgyud ba de dag ni mthar thug par rung yang srid kyi/byang chub sems dpa' bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems dang

321a8(1): byang chub sems 'di stobs bcu rnams kyi rtsa ba ste//brtan pa gzhi'i gnas yin so so shes pa'i rtsa//rgyal ba'i chos rnams bco brgyad yang dag 'byin pa ste//skyes bu gtso bos

321b2(1): shes chags med gang byung ba//kun gyi rtsa ba 'byung gnas byang chub sems 'di yin//rang rgyal theg pa 'byung zhing nyan thos theg pa'ang 'byung//bsam gtan ting 'dzin bde ba gzugs
321b3(1): kyi khams rnams dang//gzugs med khams rnams ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba dag/kun gyi rtsa ba byang chub sems mchog 'di yin no//mi ldan dang de bzhin lha'i dbang po'i bde ba dang 
321b4(1): lam rgyud sna tshogs kun kyang 'di la shin du sbyangs//brtson 'grus stobs dang dbang po'i bde ba gang yod pa//kun gyi rtsa ba dang po byang chub sems 'di yin //ci phyir zhe na 'di
321b5(1): ltar byang chub sems bskyed cing//pha rol phyin drug brtson par spyad pa rab tu spyod//sems can rnams la 'ang spyod pa yang dag rab tu ston//des ni khams gsum 'gro ba'i

321b7(1): can grol mdzad de//mya ngan 'das pa dam (pa) mchog la dgod mdzad pa//ye shes 'od gzer 'odas (kyi )yangs nyi ma lta bu ste//'gro bar (kun )spyod pa dkar pos yongs rgyas zla ba bzhin//

322a1(1): @#//zhing bde skyid chad med smon byed cing//phyi ma the'i mur thug spyod pa smon pas su brtson pa//de dag sems can 'dul ba'i don phyir rtag tu brtson//
322a2(1): 'jig rten 'bya ma glas thams cad rnam par sbyang ba'i phyir//smon lam bye ba khrag khrig bsam gyi mi khyab pa//stong pa mtshan ma med pa smon pa med dmigs pa yi//smon lam bye ba brgyas 
322a3(1): ni sems de dag par 'gyur//chos kyi rang bzhin nam mkha' lta bu sus rtogs shing//zhi dang rab zhi nyer zhir thams cad mnyam gzhog pa//de dag chos grangs khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 
322a4(1): grangs su 'gro la ston gyang de ni gnas pa med//phyogs bcu kun gyi 'jig rten gtso bo ji snyed pa//de dag kun gyis sems mchog 'di bsngags te//yon tan brgya phrag bsam gyis mi khyab pas 
322a5(1): kyang brgyan//sems can gtso bzhin yongs dag pha rol tu phyin pa yin//bskal pa sems can snyed du gal te legs brjod kyang//byang chub sems rab de la mthar thug pa yang med//
322a6(1): rgyal ba 'byung ba'i rigs chen der ni gang skyes pa//de'i dpe ni khams gsum glongs kyang gang yang med//dus gsum rgyal ba kun gyis gang zhig rnam bsags pa'i//byang chub sems rab de
322a7(1): la smon lam kun gyis gyis (da)byang chub sems rab dam pa de ni bsod nams gter//de ni rgyal sras chags pa med pa rab tu thob//'gro ba'i sems dang spyod pa shes shing mthong rung srid//

322b2(1): tshom med par 'gyur//'jig rten kun gyi bsgribs pa zad par 'gyur//'dis ni zhing rnams yongs su dag pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//'di las 'jig rten dam pa shes dang ldan pa 'byung//

322b4(1): byang chub sems bskyed bya////shin du rgyas pa chen po'i mdo'i//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las//byang chub sems dpa' sems dang po bskyed pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po'i dpe yang 
322b5(1): dag par bsags/tshigs su bcad pa'i le'u zhes bya ba ste/nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros kyis//

322b7(1): dang po bskyed pa la gnas pa//de ltar tshad med pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dang ldan pa//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rgya chen po la yang dag par 'dzegs pa//byang chub sems dpa'i
